# Agents · L · part 6

924 entries, lens-agrawal to librarian. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lens-agrawal**: Reads skills/lens-agrawal/SKILL.md and returns only that lens section for an Indian company. Use when the user asks for a QGLP, Raamdeo, or Agrawal view of an NSE or BSE company. ([shanthoshkv/the-indian-investor](https://github.com/shanthoshkv/the-indian-investor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lens-agrawal.md))
- **lens-ai-friendliness**: AI-friendliness lens for the synthetic user (#961): judges how well an agent can operate Omniglass through its CLI, API and docs, treating the explorer's own stumbles as evidence. Returns findings JSON only. ([hyperscaleav/omniglass](https://github.com/hyperscaleav/omniglass/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lens-ai-friendliness.md) · ★6)
- **lens-ai-security**: AI security lens of the production readiness audit. Treats LLM and agent integrations as attack surface - prompt injection reaching tools or system prompts, exfiltration via model output, authorization on agent actions, SSRF through model-chosen URLs, cost and token controls, model supply chain. Confirms clean absence and stops when no AI is present. Use as part of the production-readiness-audit workflow, or when reviewing an LLM feature before launch. ([Taimoorkhan1122/prod-readiness](https://github.com/Taimoorkhan1122/prod-readiness/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-ai-security.md) · ★32)
- **lens-altitude**: Checks that a spec stops above the code, leaving how each task is implemented to its coder, and stays within its size budget. ([ca77y/agentic-claude](https://github.com/ca77y/agentic-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/slopify/agents/lens-altitude.md))
- **lens-amm-hooks**: You are the AMM & HOOKS lens in a Web3 security audit. - **lens-analyst**: Applies one named analysis lens (JTBD, Opportunity Solution Tree, Morphological, Assumption Reversal, Blue Ocean, Wardley, 7 Powers or Mom Test) to a question using the run's evidence pack, citing claim ids for facts and marking the rest as judgment. Spawned by the /idea-explore skill with the lens reference path; not invoked directly. ([nextc/nextc-claude](https://github.com/nextc/nextc-claude/blob/HEAD/nextc-product/agents/lens-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **lens-analyst**: Read-only analyst for one lens group (A communication, B orchestration, C correctness, D endorsement+trend) of an improve-clauding retro. Given an inventory run dir, returns evidence-anchored candidate findings about the user's agent-usage habits. Does not rank, does not write proposals, does not modify files. ([VanillaFairy/improve-clauding](https://github.com/VanillaFairy/improve-clauding/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-analyst.md) · ★2)

- **lens-api-contract**: pr-crucible API/interface-contract lens — finds breaking changes to public interfaces (HTTP routes, RPC, CLI flags, exported library API, serialized schemas). Auto-triggers when handler/route/public-export/schema/CLI-definition files change. Emits proposed findings only. - **lens-applier**: Use when applying a single analytical lens (economic, political, historical, demographic, military, public-finance, or geographic) to one event. Designed to be invoked in parallel — one agent per lens that has been triaged as load-bearing. Returns a structured finding (claim with calibration, evidence weight, residual uncertainty, citations), not narrative. &lt;example&gt; Context: orchestrator has triaged that public-finance, economic, and political lenses are load-bearing for an Argentine capital-controls event. user: [orchestrator dispatches three lens-applier agents in parallel] assistant: lens… ([alexander-newton/anacatalyst](https://github.com/alexander-newton/anacatalyst/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-applier.md))
- **lens-architecture**: Architecture lens for review-branch. Reviews the diff for layering/boundary violations. - **lens-code**: - It starts by saying what it reviewed: the job's range, a PR, or named files. - It reports only issues it rates 80 or above for confidence, grouped as Critical (90 to 100) and Important (80 to 89). - Each finding gives the `file:line`, the repo rule or the bug it breaks, and a concrete fix. - A finding rated 90 or above shows the snippet, the input that reaches it with the wrong outcome, and why ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/content/agents/lens-code.md) · ★14)
- **lens-code**: Use this agent when you need to review code for adherence to project guidelines, style guides, and best practices. This agent should be used proactively after writing or modifying code, especially before committing changes or creating pull requests. It will check for style violations, potential issues, and ensure code follows the established patterns in the repo's constitution (AGENTS.md or CLAUDE.md, as the repo profile names it). Also the agent needs to know which files to focus on for the review. In most cases this will be recently completed work which is unstaged in git (can be retrieved… ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-code.md) · ★14)
- **lens-code**: Look for bugs and broken project rules in the changed code. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lens-code.md) · ★14)
- **lens-comments**: - It opens with a verification ledger: an entry for every comment it flags, with the code it read to check the comment's claim. - The ledger ends with a coverage line: how many comments were in scope, how many it verified, and how many it could not, with the reason. - Its findings fall under Critical Issues, Improvement Opportunities, Recommended Removals and Positive Findings, each with a `file:l ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/content/agents/lens-comments.md) · ★14)
- **lens-comments**: Check that comments and docs still match the code. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lens-comments.md) · ★14)
- **lens-comments**: Agent: `agents/lens-comments.md`. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/skills-wiki/agents/lens-comments.md) · ★14)
- **lens-comments**: Use this agent when you need to analyze code comments for accuracy, completeness, and long-term maintainability. This includes (1) after generating large documentation comments or docstrings, (2) before finalizing a change set that adds or modifies comments, (3) when reviewing existing comments for potential technical debt or comment rot, and (4) when you need to verify that comments accurately reflect the code they describe. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-comments.md) · ★14)

Deploy when you need to stress-test prevailing market views, when an investment thesis feels too comfortable, or when identifying variant perceptions that the market is mispricing. - **lens-structure**: plan을 "프로젝트 구조/아키텍처" lens로만 검토하는 서브에이전트. 판정 기준은 "이 plan이 변경 비용을 어디에 쌓는가". plan 단계에서 결정 가능한 두 규칙(R1 벌지 않은 seam, R2 지역성)을 변경비용 게이트로 적용. plan-friction-loop 스킬에서 fan-out으로 호출. ([pdev-jay/cidd-cc](https://github.com/pdev-jay/cidd-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-structure.md))
- **lens-synthesis-summarizer**: Pass-4 of the synthesis gate. Writes a single combined claim and unified fix_suggestion for grouped lens findings. Filters/scoring/dedupe are NOT in scope — that's the deterministic shell's job. ([crstamps2/agentfiles](https://github.com/crstamps2/agentfiles/blob/HEAD/common/agents/lens-synthesis-summarizer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **lens-tech-debt**: Tech debt lens for /clerk-dev:review. Looks past the changed lines into the functions, classes, and modules the change touches, and proposes cleanups, simplifications, and refactors that cut lines and make the code easier to change next time: long functions, large files, crowded directories, deep nesting, duplication, dead code, hand-rolled logic the codebase or a library already provides. Labels each finding cleanup, simplify, or refactor. Invoked by the review orchestrator, and by the design-check skill on feature records. ([clerkchat/claude-dev-plugins](https://github.com/clerkchat/claude-dev-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/clerk-dev/agents/lens-tech-debt.md))
- **lens-template**: Checks that a card draft holds every required part of its kind's template, and nothing a card must not hold. ([ca77y/agentic-claude](https://github.com/ca77y/agentic-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/slopify/agents/lens-template.md))
- **lens-tenancy**: Tenancy lens for /clerk-dev:review. Checks that every lookup, mutation, listing, and background job in the change is scoped to the calling tenant or sits behind an access guard, and that nothing leaks across tenants through caches, search, or events. Invoked by the review orchestrator, and by the design-check skill on feature records. ([clerkchat/claude-dev-plugins](https://github.com/clerkchat/claude-dev-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/clerk-dev/agents/lens-tenancy.md))
- **lens-test-coverage**: pr-crucible test-coverage lens — checks whether tests touch the changed lines and pass, and flags coverage gaps on risky paths. Always runs (light); stronger when test files or risky logic changed. Emits proposed findings only. ([AbysmalBiscuit/pr-crucible](https://github.com/AbysmalBiscuit/pr-crucible/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-test-coverage.md))
- **lens-testability**: plan을 "이대로 테스트 가능한가" 관점으로만 보는 lens. 부작용·전역상태·시간·IO 결합, 주입/관측 가능성. plan-friction-loop에서 task-gated 호출(side-effect·시간·IO·전역·주입 장애물이 미해결로 닿을 때만). ([pdev-jay/cidd-cc](https://github.com/pdev-jay/cidd-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-testability.md))
- **lens-tests**: Agent: `agents/lens-tests.md`. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/skills-wiki/agents/lens-tests.md) · ★14)
- **lens-tests**: Find changes that aren't properly tested. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lens-tests.md) · ★14)
- **lens-tests**: Review a change set for test coverage, a PR, a commit range, or the uncommitted working tree. Use after a PR or as a pre-push coverage check. Not for finding product bugs (lens-code). Not for git (the caller owns it). ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-tests.md) · ★14)
- **lens-tests**: - It states the git command that set its scope. - Each gap names the uncovered behaviour at a `file:line` and a change to the code that no test would catch. - It reports a gap only at 80 or above for confidence, and only gaps this diff introduced; older holes get one line at most. - Every recommendation uses the repo's own test harness and names the nearby test to copy. - When coverage is adequate ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/content/agents/lens-tests.md) · ★14)
- **lens-tests**: Одна линза ревью — тесты и соответствие критериям приёмки. Брать в составе доменного ревью, см. lens-contracts. ([shumik323/harness-engineering](https://github.com/shumik323/harness-engineering/blob/HEAD/skeleton/.claude/agents/lens-tests.md) · ★2)
- **lens-tests**: Tests lens for /clerk-dev:review. Judges whether the tests added or changed would catch the regressions that matter: behavioral coverage of the risky paths, negative cases, the denial paths of access control, real collaborators over mocks where the repo prefers them, flaky patterns, and tests that pin implementation instead of behavior. Invoked by the review orchestrator, and by the design-check skill on feature records. ([clerkchat/claude-dev-plugins](https://github.com/clerkchat/claude-dev-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/clerk-dev/agents/lens-tests.md))
- **lens-tests**: Review lens that checks whether a PR's tests actually prove the change. Use from the tmn-fdekit review skill on every non-trivial PR. ([teamnebula-ai/tmn-fdekit](https://github.com/teamnebula-ai/tmn-fdekit/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-tests.md))
- **lens-torres**: Read-only continuous-discovery lens: outcome at the root, one opportunity at a time, at least two solutions compared, assumptions made specific and tested. Reviews planning and product artifacts as Torres; advisory critique, never customer evidence. ([jwilger/pi-development-system](https://github.com/jwilger/pi-development-system/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-torres.md) · ★1)
- **lens-types**: - It reads the target type and two or three sibling types first, and judges against the repo's own way of expressing rules in types. - It checks the boundaries first: where database rows, request bodies or parsed JSON enter typed code without being parsed. - Each finding cites `file:line`, names the lens (boundary, expression, enforcement or usefulness), and gives a severity, a confidence and the ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/content/agents/lens-types.md) · ★14)
- **lens-types**: Check that the data types can't hold invalid values. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lens-types.md) · ★14)
- **lens-types**: Use this agent when you need expert analysis of type design in your codebase. Specifically use it (1) when introducing a new type to ensure invariants live in the type's shape and its boundaries are validated, (2) when a change set adds or modifies types, and (3) when refactoring existing types to improve their design quality. The agent returns evidence-cited findings (file:line, severity, confidence) and a single net type-health verdict. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/agents/lens-types.md) · ★14)
- **lens-types**: Agent: `agents/lens-types.md`. ([kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout](https://github.com/kaithoughtarchitect/toppingout/blob/HEAD/skills-wiki/agents/lens-types.md) · ★14)

- **lente-adversarial**: Lente adversarial de una ronda del orquestador: NO construye, intenta DEMOSTRAR que un bloque recién construido tiene un fallo grave. Úsala cuando el orquestador cierre un bloque y toque verificarlo — seguridad y acceso, corrección y cobertura, operación, o contrato con el proyecto. Parte siempre de la hipótesis de que hay al menos un fallo y lo busca con acceso de lectura al sistema real, no razonando sobre el código. Devuelve un veredicto JSON con evidencia archivo:línea y la lista de lo que intentó y no coló. NUNCA escribe archivos del repo, nunca commitea, nunca escribe en producción. - **lexcv-avaliador**: Avalia o trabalho dos outros agentes da equipa LexCV — confronta cada entrega com o contrato de entrega do próprio agente, aponta problemas, sugere melhorias concretas e atribui uma nota de 0 a 10 por dimensão e no total. Avalia o agente, não o produto. Usar depois de qualquer agente entregar, e periodicamente para detetar degradação. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-avaliador.md))
- **lexcv-coordenador**: Gestor da equipa LexCV. Mantém a lista de tarefas em .planning/TAREFAS.md, atribui cada tarefa ao agente certo, despacha-a, obriga cada entrega a passar pelo portão de revisão antes de a dar por concluída, e indica sempre o próximo passo. Não faz o trabalho — coordena quem o faz. Usar no início de sessão, para lançar trabalho novo, ou para saber o que está pendente. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-coordenador.md))
- **lexcv-editor**: Melhora um rascunho que já existe — estrutura, clareza, tom, terminologia do domínio e remoção de jargão técnico — editando o Markdown diretamente. Não inventa conteúdo novo nem verifica factos. Usar entre o lexcv-redator e o lexcv-revisor-qualidade. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-editor.md))
- **lexcv-estrategista**: Converte pesquisa e o estado real do código em decisões de produto e negócio para o LexCV — packaging, planos, pricing, limites de utilizadores, distribuição, go-to-market. Produz propostas com opções e trade-offs explícitos, não conclusões fechadas. Usar quando há uma decisão comercial ou de direção de produto a tomar. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-estrategista.md))
- **lexcv-pesquisador-negocio**: Apura factos externos sobre o mercado jurídico de Cabo Verde, regulação aplicável (proteção de dados, Ordem dos Advogados, faturação/DNRE), concorrência e benchmarks de pricing SaaS. Não decide nada — entrega factos com fonte para o lexcv-estrategista usar. Usar antes de qualquer decisão de produto, pricing ou posicionamento comercial do LexCV. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-pesquisador-negocio.md))
- **lexcv-programador**: Escreve código do LexCV que fica FORA do ciclo de fases do GSD — o site comercial em webpage/, scripts, infraestrutura (Docker, Caddy, CI) e correções pontuais que não pertencem a um marco. Nunca toca em trabalho que pertence a uma fase do .planning/ — isso é do gsd-executor. Usar para tarefas de código avulsas e bem delimitadas. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-programador.md))
- **lexcv-redator**: Produz o PRIMEIRO rascunho completo de documentos do LexCV destinados a clientes e parceiros — propostas comerciais, especificações funcionais, minutas, apresentações e o manual do utilizador — em português de Cabo Verde. Escreve Markdown como fonte e gera .docx/.pptx/.pdf a partir dele. Não revê nem pule texto de outros; para isso existem o lexcv-editor e o lexcv-revisor-qualidade. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-redator.md))
- **lexcv-revisor-qualidade**: Último portão antes de um documento do LexCV sair para cliente — verifica cada afirmação sobre o produto contra o código e o histórico de marcos, e cada número contra a sua fonte. Reporta achados com severidade e dá veredicto de bloqueio; nunca reescreve. Usar depois do lexcv-editor. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-revisor-qualidade.md))
- **lexcv-rubrica**: Ficheiro lido pelo `lexcv-avaliador`. Ajusta os pesos aqui — não dentro do agente. ([tchspprtcv/lexcv](https://github.com/tchspprtcv/lexcv/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lexcv-rubrica.md))

- **leyla**: Flow Guardian - Creative writing, UI/UX, design systems. Gate 2, 285 Hz, Water element. ([frankxai/arcanea](https://github.com/frankxai/arcanea/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/leyla.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **Leyla**: The Water Guardian - Fluid, Creative, Empathetic ([frankxai/arcanea](https://github.com/frankxai/arcanea/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/%40ley-la.agent.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **lf-builder**: Default worker for one scoped Life Framework issue end to end (worktree, red-proof test, fix, validate, wt ship). Also visual builds, asset pipeline items, placement (one area per agent), video cuts, QA flows, website, tools, and splitting epics into issues. ([Life-Framework/Life-Framework](https://github.com/Life-Framework/Life-Framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lf-builder.md) · ★5)
- **lf-critico**: Crítico independiente de LegacyForge, de solo lectura. Úsalo antes de presentar una propuesta con señales de rigor (dinero, datos, contratos, difícil de revertir, regla repetida): recibe la propuesta y su evidencia e intenta tumbarla buscando evidencia débil, alternativas más simples, consumidores u otros lugares de la regla olvidados, falta de encaje con el sistema y escenarios de falla. ([thejoemr/legacy-forge](https://github.com/thejoemr/legacy-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/lf-critico.md))
- **lf-escenarios**: Simulador de escenarios de LegacyForge, de solo lectura. Úsalo para recorrer un flujo bajo condiciones distintas sin ejecutarlo: entradas, estados, concurrencia, tiempo y contextos de entorno (varios servidores, balanceador, reciclado, caídas). Responde una duda acotada, por ejemplo por qué algo falla solo a veces o si un cambio aguanta un caso, y devuelve los escenarios que la resuelven con su receta para forzarlos. ([thejoemr/legacy-forge](https://github.com/thejoemr/legacy-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/lf-escenarios.md))
- **lf-explorador**: Explorador de solo lectura de LegacyForge. Úsalo para localizar y resumir: seguir un flujo por el código, encontrar todos los consumidores de algo, ubicar dónde más vive una regla de negocio, encontrar el porqué de un fragmento en sus comentarios o revisar qué cambió en el historial. Devuelve hallazgos con ruta y línea. ([thejoemr/legacy-forge](https://github.com/thejoemr/legacy-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/lf-explorador.md))
- **lf-hard**: Opus escalation for Life Framework work Sonnet could not finish, or that is hard from the start - replication/JIP, persistence and restart bugs, new-system design (the plan, not the build), risky diff review (interrogate). Use only when lf-builder failed twice or the job is clearly hard; never for visual, asset, placement, video or QA builds. ([Life-Framework/Life-Framework](https://github.com/Life-Framework/Life-Framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lf-hard.md) · ★5)
- **lf-scout**: Cheap read-only lookups in the Life Framework repo. Enfusion API or asset lookups (tools\cli call api_search|asset_search|game_read), grepping logs, PR/CI status, summarising one file or thread. Returns a short answer, never edits. ([Life-Framework/Life-Framework](https://github.com/Life-Framework/Life-Framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lf-scout.md) · ★5)
- **lf-simulador**: Simulador de uso de LegacyForge. Úsalo para probar el paquete antes de liberarlo: monta un proyecto heredado de prueba, interpreta al desarrollador y al copiloto aplicando las skills con criterio, y reporta dónde las skills fallan, se contradicen, no dicen qué hacer o empujan al copiloto a comportarse como un procedimiento. No corrige las skills: reporta. ([thejoemr/legacy-forge](https://github.com/thejoemr/legacy-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/lf-simulador.md))
- **lf-verificador**: Verificador independiente de LegacyForge, de solo lectura. Úsalo para comprobar contra el código que un cambio aplicado hace lo que dice y nada más, o que una afirmación sobre el sistema es cierta, y para diseñar casos de prueba que intenten romper un cambio de riesgo alto. ([thejoemr/legacy-forge](https://github.com/thejoemr/legacy-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/lf-verificador.md))
- **lfg-developer**: Task execution agent that implements planned tasks with atomic commits. Use for executing individual tasks from a milestone plan. ([dgca/mai](https://github.com/dgca/mai/blob/HEAD/plugins/lfg/agents/developer.md) · ★3)
- **lfg-explorer**: Fast codebase exploration agent for mapping files, identifying patterns, and surfacing concerns. Use for quick reconnaissance before planning or execution. ([dgca/mai](https://github.com/dgca/mai/blob/HEAD/plugins/lfg/agents/explorer.md) · ★3)
- **lfg-pm**: Project Manager agent for breaking down epics into milestones and milestones into tasks. Use for planning work with proper dependencies and requirements tracking. ([dgca/mai](https://github.com/dgca/mai/blob/HEAD/plugins/lfg/agents/pm.md) · ★3)
- **lfg-qa**: Quality assurance agent that independently verifies completed tasks. Never trusts — always verifies. ([dgca/mai](https://github.com/dgca/mai/blob/HEAD/plugins/lfg/agents/qa.md) · ★3)
- **lfg-researcher**: Deep research agent for investigating technologies, APIs, and approaches before planning. Use when research_depth is set to "deep" or specific research is needed. ([dgca/mai](https://github.com/dgca/mai/blob/HEAD/plugins/lfg/agents/researcher.md) · ★3)
- **lfm-firefly**: Firefly Velvet-Rope Closer subagent. Engineers the coded conversion path from attention to booked call or 1-click checkout, pre-handles buyer objections, respects founder calendar capacity, and feeds conversion telemetry back to RBM. ([cynthiaschomp/lightning-firefly-model](https://github.com/cynthiaschomp/lightning-firefly-model/blob/HEAD/agents/firefly_closer.md))
- **lfm-lightning**: Lightning Rainmaker subagent. Audits and generates high-voltage buyer-attracting hooks, carousels, reels, ad angles, and forensic teardowns across the 7-Bolt Voltage Spectrum using LFM_PROFILE.md and RBM_PROFILE.md. ([cynthiaschomp/lightning-firefly-model](https://github.com/cynthiaschomp/lightning-firefly-model/blob/HEAD/agents/lightning_rainmaker.md))
- **lfm-synthesizer**: Lightning Firefly Council Coordinator. Runs Lightning (The Rainmaker) and Firefly (The Velvet-Rope Closer) in tandem, synchronizes with Rainbow Butterfly (RBM_PROFILE.md & RBM_LEDGER.md), and updates LFM_PIPELINE.md. ([cynthiaschomp/lightning-firefly-model](https://github.com/cynthiaschomp/lightning-firefly-model/blob/HEAD/agents/lfm_synthesizer.md))
